    Company Profile
    公司簡介

    WEILONG Delicious Global Holdings Ltd (the “Company”) and its                  衛龍美味全球控股有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司
    subsidiaries (“the Group”, “we” or “us”) are a leading spicy snack food       （「本集團」或「我們」）是中國領先的集研發、生產及銷
    enterprise integrating R&D, production and sales in China. The Group was       售為一體的辣味休閒食品企業。本集團於2022年12月
    listed on the Main Board of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange (stock code:          15日在香港聯交所主板上市（股份代號：9985.HK）。
    9985.HK) on December 15, 2022.

    Based on traditional formulas, the Group started the Chinese seasoned         本集團以傳統美食為基礎開創了中國調味面製品（俗
    flour products (also commonly known as Latiao (辣條)) industry, created         稱「辣條」）行業，於2001年開創出第一根辣條，並引
    our first Latiao (辣條) snack in 2001, leading the standards construction of    領了辣條行業標準建設。憑藉出眾的產品拓展能力，
    Latiao industry. With our outstanding product development capabilities,       本集團已成功擴展至蔬菜製品、豆製品及其它產品等
    the Group has successfully diversified into vegetable products, bean-         品類，推出了包括魔芋爽、風吃海帶、小魔女等大單
    based products and others, including Konjac Shuang (魔芋爽), Fengchi             品。 本年度，本集團又推出爆麻爆辣味「麻辣麻辣」、
    Kelp (風吃海帶), XIAO MO NV (小魔女) and others. The Group has further               爆辣素毛肚「魔芋爽」等新品，深受消費者的喜愛。
    launched new products like explosively spicy Mala Mala (爆麻爆辣味麻
    辣麻辣), explosively spicy vegetarian tripe Konjac Shuang (爆辣素毛肚
    魔芋爽) and others in the Current Year, which are quite popular among
    consumers.

    Adhering to our product development philosophy of “maximizing the             秉承「以工業化思維，讓美食原點的價值最大化釋放」
    intrinsic value of nature with an industrialized approach”, the Group         的產品開發理念，本集團持續升級現有產品及推出新
    continuously upgrades existing products and launches new products,            產品，專注品質，建立了行業領先的生產體系及嚴格
    focuses on quality through establishing an industry-leading production        的品質保障體系。
    system and strict quality assurance system.

    We are a popular snack food brand among young consumers in China.             我們是倍受中國年輕消費者喜愛的休閒食品品牌，本
    The Group focuses and understands the needs of young consumer                 集團深度洞察年輕消費群體的需求，不斷運用新穎有
    groups, constantly enhances brand value through novel and interesting         趣的互動玩法提升品牌價值，並緊跟時下熱點話題趨
    interactions with consumers, and keeps up with current hot topic trends       勢，賦予品牌新活力。本年度，我們榮登胡潤百富頒
    to give the brand new vitality. In 2024, we have won more than 20             發的「胡潤國潮品牌百強榜」，榮獲每日經濟新聞頒
    awards, including “Hurun China Top 100 Domestic Brands List” by Hurun         發的「2024食品飲料行業創新力榜『Z世代』喜愛品牌
    Report, “ 2024 Food & Beverage Sector Innovation Power List -Top 10           TOP10」及「2024大消費最具成長公司」以及由中國廣
    Brands Favored by Generation Z” and “2024 Most Promising Growth               告協會頒發的「第31屆中國國際廣告節青年榜2024大
    Companies in the Consumer Sector” by National Business Daily and the          學生喜愛的活力品牌」等二十餘項獎項。
    “The 31st China International Advertising Festival Youth List -2024 Vibrant
    Brands Favored by College Students” by China Advertising Association.

    The Group has an omni-channel sales and distribution network that             本集團擁有有效觸達年輕消費者的全渠道銷售及經銷
    effectively reaches consumers, we have quickly adapted to the evolving        網絡，緊隨消費者購買行為的演變，我們迅速擴張到
    purchasing behavior of consumers and expanded to supermarkets,                廣泛覆蓋消費者的商超、連鎖便利店、傳統電商及新
    chained convenience stores, traditional e-commerce channels and               興的零食量販店、內容電商、社交電商等渠道。
    emerging snacks specialty retailers, content e-commerce channels
    and social e-commerce channels which have wide exposure to young
    consumers.

We adhere to the mission “to let the world fall in love with Chinese         我們秉承「讓世界人人愛上中國味」的重大使命，持續
flavors” and are continuously committed to the good vision of “making        致力於讓「傳統美食娛樂化、休閒化、便捷化、親民
authentic Chinese gourmet more entertaining, casual, convenient,             化、數據化、自動化，樂活123年的生態平台」的美好
affordable, data-enabled and automated, ultimately build a great business    願景，堅持「本分，創新，客戶第一，員工為本」的核
that brings joy and happiness to people for 123 years”. We adhere to the     心價值觀，持續不斷地為消費者提供更好的產品和愉
core value of “Integrity, Innovation, Customers First, Employee Oriented”,   悅的消費體驗。
continuously offer consumers better products and a cheerful consumption
experience.

Management Discussion and Analysis
管理層討論與分析

MACRO AND INDUSTRY ENVIRONMENT                                                          宏觀及行業環境
Looking back at 2024, in the face of a complex and challenging domestic                 回顧2024年，面對錯綜複雜的國內外環境，中國經
and international environment, China’s economy maintained overall                       濟運行總體平穩，實現了全年5%的增長，充分展現
stability, achieving 5% growth for the year. This fully demonstrated the                了國內經濟在內外部挑戰下的韌性和潛能。經濟的穩
resilience and potential of the domestic economy amidst both internal and               步復甦與消費信心的逐步回升，為市場注入了新的活
external challenges. The steady economic recovery and gradual rebound                   力。
in consumer confidence have injected new vitality into the market.


China’s consumer market, with its vast scale and dynamic growth,                        中國消費市場以其龐大的規模和快速的增長動能，持
continues to play a central role in driving economic growth. Shaped by                  續在經濟增長中扮演著核心角色。受全球經濟大背景
the global economic landscape and China’s own economic development,                     與中國經濟發展狀況影響，中國消費市場不斷呈現出
the Chinese consumer market is constantly evolving with new and rapid                   新的快速變化。消費需求、消費理念以及消費行為模
changes. Consumer needs, preferences, and behaviour patterns are                        式日益分化，在這個「分需時代1」，更多消費者的消費
becoming increasingly fragmented. This “era of fragmented needs ”                 1
                                                                                        觀念趨向「理性」和「悅己」，他們越來越關注購買高品
sees a growing number of consumers embracing a “rational” and “self-                    質且價格合適的產品，特別是年輕一代作為消費市場
indulgent” approach to consumption. They prioritize the purchase of                     的中堅力量與潮流的引領者，他們追求精緻生活、注
high-quality and reliable products. In particular, the younger generation,              重消費體驗以及個性化的消費，品牌意識亦在逐步增
as key influencers and trendsetters in the consumer market, are especially              強，這些新的趨勢都為消費品牌帶來了更多新的結構
focused on sophisticated lifestyles, exceptional consumer experiences                   性機會，並推動著品牌的持續創新，從而快速響應市
and personalized consumption. Their brand awareness is also gradually                   場和消費者的變化。
strengthening. These emerging trends are creating more new structural
business opportunities for consumer brands and driving continuous
innovation, enabling brands to respond quickly to market and consumer
changes.


On July 30, 2024, a meeting of the Political Bureau of the CPC Central                  2024年7月30日，中共中央政治局會議強調將經濟政
Committee emphasized shifting the focus of economic policy more                         策的著力點更多轉向改善民生和促進消費。在此背景
towards improving people’s livelihoods and boosting consumption.                        下，食品飲料行業呈現出強勁的復甦勢頭。根據沙利
Against this backdrop, the food and beverage industry was showing                       文和每日食品聯合發佈的《食為天：2024中國食品飲
a robust recovery. The “Food is Paramount: 2024 Chinese Food and                        料行業白皮書》2中指出，中國休閒零食市場在年輕人
Beverage Industry White Paper2,” a joint publication by Frost & Sullivan                休閒需求的驅動下，正通過持續的創新和精準化營銷
and Foodaily, indicates that China’s casual snack food market, driven                   實現穩定增長。同時，量販零食、線上內容電商等作
by the leisure needs of young consumers, is experiencing steady growth                  為新興的休閒食品零售形態，以多品牌、多品類和高
through continuous innovation and targeted marketing strategies.                        性價比的優勢火速崛起並快速增長，驅動著渠道的變
Meanwhile, emerging retail formats for snack foods, such as snack                       革。面對這樣一個日新月異的市場格局，休閒食品品
specialty retailers, and online content-driven e-commerce, are experiencing             牌惟有積極擁抱變化，加速創新產品與服務，從而在
rapid expansion and high-growth rates due to their advantages in offering               消費中搶佔先機。
a wide range of brands, diverse product selections, and excellent value for
money, accelerating the evolution of sales channels. Facing such a rapidly
evolving market landscape, consumer brands must actively embrace
changes and accelerate the innovation of products and services so as to

OUR FOOD SAFETY AND QUALITY CONTROL                                           我們的食品安全和品質控制
As one of China’s leading snack food enterprises, we always take quality      作為中國領先的休閒食品企業之一，我們始終以質量
as our core and regard food safety as the “lifeline” of our enterprise. We    為核心，將食品安全視為企業的「生命線」，不斷完善
continuously improve and upgrade our food safety management system.           和升級食品安全管理體系。我們堅守產品品質，從原
We adhere to product quality and strictly control every aspect from raw       料選取到生產全程嚴格把控，確保為消費者提供安全
material selection to the entire production process to ensure that we         可靠的產品。同時，我們積極推動創新研發，以更健
provide consumers with safe and reliable products. At the same time,          康、更美味的休閒食品滿足消費者日益多樣化的需
we actively promote innovation and research and development, and aim          求，致力於用優質產品贏得市場的信任與支持。
to meet consumers’ increasingly diverse needs with healthier and more
delicious snack foods. We are committed to winning the trust and support
of the market with high-quality products.


The Group established a sound food safety and quality assurance               本集團建立了健全的全鏈條、全生命週期的食品安全
system of the whole chain and the whole product life cycle. The system        和品質保障體系。該體系覆蓋了產品生產的全過程，
covering the whole process of production, covering R&D, procurement,          覆蓋研發、採購、生產、儲存、經銷到銷售等環節，
manufacturing, storage, distribution to sales and other links. Throughout     在整個生產過程中使用HACCP危害分析與關鍵控制點
the production process, the entire production process can be controllable     確保整個生產過程可控，在檢驗放行方面執行嚴格的
by using the HACCP hazard analysis and critical control points. In terms of   《產品檢驗與放行控制程序》；在檢測能力方面，通過
product inspection and release, “Product Inspection and Release Control       精端的檢測設備HPLC-MS、GC-MS、ICP-MS等針對
Procedures” are strictly implemented; in terms of detection capacity,         食品添加劑、重金屬等指標進行嚴格監控；在風險管
HPLC-MS, GC-MS, ICP-MS and other high-end detection equipments                理方面，通過《食品安全信息收集及預警》、《食品安
are applied to strictly monitor the food additives, heavy metals and other    全監測及風險評估規範》、《食品安全自查管理制度》
indicators; in terms of risk management, advance prevention is carried        等制度進行事前預防。在新產品風險管理方面，依據
out through the “Food Safety Information Collection and Early Warning”,       《新品品質驗證標準》進行風險評估。
“Food Safety Monitoring and Risk Assessment Norms”, “Food Safety
Self-inspection Management System” and other systems. In terms of risk
management of new products, risk assessment is carried out under “New
Product Quality Inspection Standards”.


The Group has also set up a quality management department, which              本集團亦設立了品質管理部門，主要負責原材料與包
is mainly responsible for raw materials and packaging materials               裝材料管理、生產過程管理和產品檢驗管理、代工廠
management, production process management and product testing                 以及客戶服務管理以及食品安全和品質保障，全力打
management, OEM and customer service management and food safety               造源頭掌控、生產管控、認證嚴控、售後把控的閉環
and quality assurance, and strives to build a closed-loop management          管理體系，從而為消費者提供最優質的產品。
system of source control, production control, strict certification control

     Treasury policy                                                              庫務政策
     The Group adopts a conservative financial management approach for            本集團針對其庫務政策採取保守的財務管理方法，以
     its treasury policy to ensure a sustainable working capital management       確保實現可持續的營運資金管理，即集團的流動性結
     that the Group’s liquidity structure comprising assets, liabilities and      構（包括資產、負債及其他承擔）能夠滿足集團的運營
     other commitments that fulfilling the Group operations requirements          需求及其資本承諾。此外，集團會定期審查資金管理
     and its capital commitments. In addition, the Group reviews the treasury     政策，以強化其投資組合策略，尤其是在基金管理產
     management policies on a regular basis so as to strengthen its investment    品方面，從而將投資風險降至最低，並保護集團的利
     portfolio strategy, particularly in relation with the fund management        益。
     products to minimize the investment risk and protect the interests of the
     Group.


     FOREIGN EXCHANGE RISK                                                        外匯風險
     The Group mainly operates in China with most of the transactions settled     本集團主要在中國經營，大部分交易乃以人民幣結
     in RMB. The functional currency of the subsidiaries in mainland is RMB,      算。內地附屬公司的功能貨幣為人民幣，而本公司及
     while the functional currency of the Company and subsidiaries outside        其中國內地以外附屬公司的功能貨幣為港元、美元或
     mainland of China is HKD, USD or IDR. Both the entities in and outside       印尼盾。位於中國內地及境外的實體均有資產及負
     mainland of China have assets and liabilities, like cash at bank and other   債，如於銀行及其他主要持牌支付機構的現金、受

     RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                         風險管理及內部控制
     The Board is aware of its responsibility for risk management and internal    董事會知悉其有關風險管理及內部控制系統，並至少
     control systems and reviews their effectiveness at least once annually.      每年審閱一次其有效性的責任。該系統旨在管理而非
     The system is designed to manage, rather than eliminate, the risk of         消除無法實現業務目標的風險，促進有效及高效運
     failure to achieve business objectives, to promote effective and efficient   營，合理保證財務報告可靠性及遵守適用法律及法規
     operations, to reasonably guarantee the reliability of financial reporting   以及保障本公司資產，且僅可就重大錯報或損失提供
     and compliance with applicable laws and regulations, and to safeguard        合理而非絕對的保證。
     the assets of the Company, and the system only to provide a reasonable,
     but not absolute, guarantee against material misstatement or loss.


     The Board has overall responsibility for assessing and determining           董事會全面負責評估及釐定其在實現本公司策略目標
     the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to take in achieving        及建立及維護適宜且有效的風險管理及內部控制系統
     the Company’s strategic objectives and establishing and maintaining          方面願意承擔的風險的性質和程度。
     appropriate and effective risk management and internal control systems.


     Characteristics of the Risk Management and Internal                          風險管理和內部監控組織體系特點
     Control Organization System
     In accordance with the requirements of Code Provision D.2.2 under            根據《企業管治守則》第二部分第D.2.2條的要求，為
     Section II of the Corporate Governance Code, the Company has established     確保本公司在風險管理和內部審核方面具備足夠的資
     a sound risk management and internal control organization system which       源、員工資歷及經驗、足夠的培訓課程和有關預算，
     includes the Board, the Audit Committee, the office of the Board, the        本公司建立了完善的風險管理和內部監控組織體系，
     audit center, the internal control department, the legal department and      包括董事會、審核委員會、董事會辦公室、審計中
     other departments to ensure that the Company has sufficient resources,       心、內控部、法務部以及其他各部門。
     qualified and experienced staffs, training courses and related budget for
     risk management and internal audit.


     The Board of Directors, as the risk management supreme leading organ         董事會是本公司風險管理的最高領導機構，對整體風
     of the Company, undertakes ultimate responsibility for establishing,         險管理體系的建設和有效運行負最終責任，其負責整
     improvement and effective operation of the overall risk management           體風險管理體系的建立、健全和有效運轉，並負責明
     system, takes charge of the building, improvement and efficient operation    確高級管理層、各部門在風險管理體系中的職責分
     of the overall risk management system and has the discretion to delegate     工。
     senior management and operational division of each department in the
     risk management system.


     The Board of Directors is responsible for evaluating and determining the     董事會負責評估及釐定本公司達成戰略目標時所願意
     nature and extent of the risks the Company is willing to take in achieving   接納的風險性質及程度，並確保本公司設立及維持合

PRINCIPAL BUSINESS                                                           主要業務
The Group is a leading spicy snack food company in China, mainly             本集團是中國領先的辣味休閒食品企業，主要在中國
engaged in the production and sales of spicy snack food in China.            從事辣味休閒食品的生產和銷售。本集團的主要產品
The Group’s major products cover seasoned flour products, vegetable          覆蓋調味麵製品、蔬菜製品、豆製品及其他產品等類
products, bean-based and other products, etc.                                別。

accompanied the youthhood of the millennials in China, who became            的成長，同時也為衛龍積累了最早的一批忠實粉絲。
our first loyal customers. Meanwhile, under the product development          同時，秉承「以工業化思維，讓美食原點的價值最大
philosophy of “maximizing the intrinsic value of nature with an              化釋放」的產品開發理念，憑藉我們出眾的品類拓展
industrialized approach”, and with our outstanding category development      能力，我們已成功拓展到蔬菜製品和豆製品，我們新
ability, we have diversified into vegetable products and bean-based          潮且深受消費者青睞的產品，滿足了一代又一代人的
products. Our modern and well received products have satisfied the taste     味蕾，積累了橫跨多個年齡段的廣泛客戶群。
buds of generations and accumulated a broad and customer base across
various age groups.


We constantly adapt to changing purchasing behavior of consumers and         我們與時俱進，隨著消費者購買行為的轉變，迅速擴
have quickly expanded to supermarkets, chained convenience stores,           張到年輕消費者廣泛接觸的商超、連鎖便利店、電商

     To the shareholders of WEILONG Delicious Global Holdings Ltd                     致衛龙美味全球控股有限公司股東

     OPINION                                                                         意見
     We have audited the consolidated financial statements of WEILONG                我們已審計衛龙美味全球控股有限公司（以下簡稱「貴
     Delicious Global Holdings Ltd (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the         公司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）列載於第
     “Group”) set out on pages 105 to 216, which comprise the consolidated           105至216頁的合併財務報表，包括於2024年12月31
     statement of financial position as at 31 December 2024, and the                 日的合併財務狀況表、合併損益表、合併綜合收益
     consolidated statement of profit or loss, the consolidated statement of         表、合併權益變動表及截至該日止年度的合併現金流
     comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of changes in equity           量表，以及合併財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策信
     and the consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then ended,           息。
     and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including material
     accounting policy information.


     In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and           我們認為，該等合併財務報表已根據國際會計準則理
     fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31          事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒佈的《國際財務報告
     December 2024, and of its consolidated financial performance and its            準則》會計準則真實而中肯地反映了     貴集團於2024
     consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance with IFRS         年12月31日的合併財務狀況及其截至該日止年度的合
     Accounting Standards as issued by the International Accounting Standards        併財務表現及合併現金流量，並已遵照香港《公司條
     Board (“IASB”) and have been properly prepared in compliance with the           例》的披露規定妥為擬備。
     disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.


     BASIS FOR OPINION                                                               意見的基礎

      users taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.         誤陳述可被視作重大。

1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                                     1. 一般資料

    company with limited liability under the Company Act (Cap 22, Law       （1961年第3號法例，經合併及修訂）於開曼群島
    3 of 1961 as consolidated and revised) of the Cayman Islands. The        註冊成立為豁免有限公司。其註冊辦事處位於
    address of its registered office is the offices of Maples Corporate      Maples Corporate Services Limited的辦事處（地
    Services Limited, PO Box 309, Ugland House, Grand Cayman, KY1-           址為PO Box 309, Ugland House, Grand Cayman,
    1104, Cayman Islands.                                                    KY1-1104, Cayman Islands）。

    The Company is an investment holding company. The Company and            本公司為一家投資控股公司。本公司及其附屬公
    its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) are principally engaged in      司（統稱「本集團」）主要在中華人民共和國（「中
    the production and sale of spicy snack food in the People’s Republic     國」）從事辣味休閒食品的生產和銷售。
    of China (“PRC”).


    The ultimate holding company of the Company is HH Global Capital         本公司的最終控股公司為和和全球資本有限公
    Ltd. The ultimate controlling parties are Mr. Liu Weiping and his        司。最終控制方為劉衛平先生及其弟弟劉福平先
    brother Mr. Liu Fuping (the “Controlling Shareholders”), who are         生（「控股股東」），彼等亦分別為本公司董事會主

           2.1 Basis of preparation                                                       2.1 編製基準
                 The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in                合併財務報表乃按照國際會計準則及常務
                 accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards and interpretations              詮釋委員會頒佈的適用於根據《國際財務報
                 as issued by International Accounting Standards and Standing               告準則》會計準則進行報告的公司的《國際
                 Interpretations Committee applicable to companies reporting                財務報告準則》會計準則及詮釋編製。財務
                 under IFRS Accounting Standards. The financial statements                  報表符合國際會計準則理事會（「國際會計
                 comply with IFRS Accounting Standards as issued by the                     準則理事會」）頒佈的《國際財務報告準則》
                 International Accounting Standards Board (“IASB”). The                     會計準則。合併財務報表根據歷史成本慣
                 consolidated financial statements have been prepared under                 例進行編製，並通過對以公允價值計量且
                 the historical cost convention, as modified by the revaluation of          其變動計入當期損益的若干金融資產重新
                 certain financial assets at fair value through profit or loss. These       估值進行修改。除另有說明外，該等合併
                 consolidated financial statements are presented in Renminbi                財務報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列及四捨
                 (“RMB”) and rounded to nearest thousand, unless otherwise                  五入至最接近的千元。
                 stated.


                 The preparation of consolidated financial statements in                    根據《國際財務報告準則》會計準則編製合
                 conformity with IFRS Accounting Standards requires the                     併財務報表需使用若干關鍵會計估計，同
                 use of certain critical accounting estimates. It also requires             時要求管理層在應用本集團會計政策過程
                 management to exercise its judgement in the process of                     中作出判斷。涉及高度判斷或複雜性的方
                 applying the Group’s accounting policies. The areas involving              面或假設及估計對合併財務報表屬重大的
                 a higher degree of judgement or complexity, or areas where                 方面於附註4內披露。
                 assumptions and estimates are significant to the consolidated
                 financial statements are disclosed in Note 4.


           2.2 Changes in accounting policies and disclosures                             2.2 會計政策及披露事項變動
                 The Group has adopted the following revised IFRS Accounting                本集團已就目前年度財務報表，首次採納
                 Standards for the first time for the current year’s financial              下列經修訂
                                                                                                《國際財務報告準則》
                                                                                                         會計準則。
                 statements.

              are fully consolidated from the date on which control is                  體的控制權。附屬公司自控制權轉移

              the date that control ceases.                                             終止之日起停止合併。

              the Group.


              Intercompany transactions, balances and unrealized                        已將公司間交易、集團各公司間交易

              eliminated. Unrealized losses are also eliminated unless                  供了所轉讓資產減值的證據，否則還

              the acquiree. For each business combination, the Group                     集團選擇是否以公允價值或按被收購

              incurred.


              The Group determines that it has acquired a business                       當所收購的一組活動及資產包括一項

              conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the                   包括分開被收購方主合同中的嵌入衍

              the acquiree.

              recognized in profit or loss. Contingent consideration that                權益的或有對價並無重新計量，而其
              is classified as equity is not remeasured and subsequent                   後結算於權益中入賬。
              settlement is accounted for within equity.

                       Group are assigned to those units or groups of units.

        Revenue from contracts with customers                                   來自客戶合同的收入
        Revenue from contracts with customers is recognized when                來自客戶合同的收入於貨品的控制權已按
        control of goods is transferred to the customers at an amount           可反映本集團預期有權就交換該等貨品所
        that reflects the consideration to which the Group expects to           得對價的金額轉讓予客戶時確認。
        be entitled in exchange for those goods.


        When the consideration in a contract includes a variable                當合同的對價包含可變金額時，估計對價
        amount, the amount of consideration is estimated to which               金額為本集團向客戶轉讓貨品而有權換取
        the Group will be entitled in exchange for transferring the             的金額。可變對價於合同開始時估計並受
        goods to the customer. The variable consideration is estimated          到約束，直至與可變對價相關的不確定因
        at contract inception and constrained until it is highly probable       素其後得到解決時，已確認累計收入金額
        that a significant revenue reversal in the amount of cumulative         極有可能不會發生重大收入撥回。由於有
        revenue recognized will not occur when the associated                   關銷售按預付款項作出或其信貸期不超過
        uncertainty with the variable consideration is subsequently             90日，故不被視為存在重大融資要素。
        resolved. No significant element of financing is deemed present
        as the sales are made with a prepayment or a credit term up to
        90 days.

              The costs of transporting finished goods to a customer                  時於經銷及銷售費用中確認。
              are recognized in distribution and selling expenses when
              occurred.


              Some contracts for the sale of goods provide customers                  部分銷售貨品合同為客戶提供退貨權

    2.18 Leases                                                                 2.18 租賃
        Leases are recognized as a right-of-use asset and a                        租賃於租賃資產可供本集團使用之日確認
        corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset is           為使用權資產及有關負債。
        available for use by the Group.


        Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components.                 合同可能包括租賃和非租賃部分。本集團
        The Group allocates the consideration in the contract to the               將合同中的對價按相對獨立價格基準分配
        lease and non-lease components based on their relative stand-              至租賃及非租賃部分。然而，就本集團作
        alone prices. However, for leases of real estate for which the             為承租人的房地產租賃而言，本集團已選
        Group is a lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-           擇不分開租賃和非租賃部分，而是將彼等
        lease components and instead accounts for these as a single                作為單一租賃部分入賬。
        lease component.


        Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured         賃產生的資產及負債初步按現值基準計
        on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present        量。租賃負債包括以下租賃付款之淨現值：
        value of the following lease payments:


        •     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments),              •   固定付款（包括實質固定付款）減任何
              less any lease incentives receivable                                     應收租賃獎勵

        •     variable lease payment that are based on an index or a               •   基於指數或利率的可變租賃付款，最
              rate, initially measured using the index or rate as at the               初以租賃起始日的指數或利率計量
              commencement date


        •     amounts expected to be payable by the Group under                    •   預期本集團於剩餘價值擔保下應付的
              residual value guarantees                                                款項

        •     the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is              •   購買選擇權的行使價（倘本集團合理
              reasonably certain to exercise that option, and                          確定將行使該項選擇權），及

        •     payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the              •   支付終止租賃的罰款（倘租期反映本
              lease term reflects the Group exercising that option.                    集團行使該項選擇權）。

        Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension               根據合理確定延期選擇權作出的租賃付款

                 The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate                租賃付款採用租賃有關的隱含利率貼現。
                 implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily determined,        如果該利率無法直接釐定（本集團的租賃通

                 incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the              現，即個人承租人在類似的經濟環境下，
                 individual lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds                  以類似的條款、擔保和條件獲得與使用權
                 necessary to obtain an asset of similar value to the right-of-           資產價值相近的資產所需支付的借款利率。
                 use asset in a similar economic environment with similar terms,
                 security and conditions.


                 To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                  為釐定增量借款利率，本集團：

                      by the individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to                第三方融資為出發點進行調整，以反
                      reflect changes in financing conditions since third party                映自獲得第三方融資以來融資條件的
                      financing was received                                                   變動

                 •    uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free                •   使用累加法，首先就本公司所持有的
                      interest rate adjusted for credit risk for leases held by                租賃的信貸風險（最近並無第三方融
                      the company, which does not have recent third-party                      資）調整無風險利率，及
                      financing, and

                      country, currency and security.                                          家、貨幣及抵押。

                 lease payments based on an index or rate, which are not                  租賃付款，而有關指數或利率在生效前不

           2.23 Fair value measurement                                                     2.23 公允價值計量
                 The Group measures certain of its financial assets and financial            本集團於報告期末按公允價值計量其若干
                 liabilities at fair value at the end of the reporting period. Fair          金融資產及金融負債。公允價值為市場參
                 value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or               與者於計量日期在有序交易中出售資產所
                 paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between              收取的價格或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公
                 market participants at the measurement date. The fair value                 允價值計量乃根據假設出售資產或轉讓負
                 measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction                債的交易於資產或負債主要市場或（在無主
                 to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the       要市場情況下）最具優勢市場進行而作出。
                 principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a         主要或最具優勢市場須為本集團可進入的
                 principal market, in the most advantageous market for the asset             市場。資產或負債的公允價值乃按假設市
                 or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market                 場參與者於資產或負債定價時會以最佳經
                 must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset                 濟利益行事計量。
                 or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market
                 participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
                 assuming that market participants act in their economic best
                 interest.


                 The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in                 本集團採用適合具體情況並且有足夠可用
                 the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to            數據支持的估值技術，並盡可能多地使用
                 measure fair value, maximizing the use of relevant observable               相關可觀察輸入值，盡可能少地使用不可
                 inputs and minimizing the use of unobservable inputs, see Note              觀察輸入值（見下文附註3.3）。
                 3.3 below.

3. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (Cont’d)                                               3. 財務風險管理（續）
    3.3 Fair value estimation                                                         3.3 公允價值估計
        (a)   Fair value hierarchy                                                        (a)   公允價值層級
              To provide an indication about the reliability of the inputs                      為得出有關釐定公允價值所用輸入數
              used in determining fair value, the Group has classified                          據之可靠性指標，本集團已按會計準
              its financial instruments into the three levels prescribed                        則規定將其金融工具分為三個層級。
              under the accounting standards. An explanation of each                            下表為對各層級的說明。
              follows the table below.


              Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments traded in                        第一層級：在活躍市場買賣的金融工
              active markets (such as publicly traded derivatives, and                          具（例如公開買賣的衍生工具及股本
              equity securities) is based on quoted market prices at the                        證券）的公允價值根據報告期末的市
              end of the reporting period. The quoted market price used                         場報價列賬。本集團持有的金融資產
              for financial assets held by the Group is the current bid                         所使用的市場報價為現時買盤價。該
              price. These instruments are included in level 1.                                 等工具計入第一層級。

              Level 2: The fair value of financial instruments that                             第二層級：並非於活躍市場買賣的金
              are not traded in an active market (for example, over-                            融工具（例如場外衍生工具）的公允價
              the-counter derivatives) is determined using valuation                            值乃使用估值技術釐定，該等估值技
              techniques which maximize the use of observable market                            術盡量利用可觀察市場數據，並盡可
              data and rely as little as possible on entity-specific                            能減少依賴實體特定的估計。倘計算
              estimates. If all significant inputs required to fair value an                    工具公允價值所需的所有重大輸入數
              instrument are observable, the instrument is included in                          據均屬可觀察，則將工具計入第二層
              level 2.                                                                          級。

              Level 3: If one or more of the significant inputs are                             第三層級：倘一項或多項重大輸入數
              not based on observable market data, the instrument                               據並非按可觀察市場數據得出，則該

4. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                      4. 重大會計估計及判斷
   JUDGEMENTS
    Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated. They are based         持續評估估計及判斷。估計及判斷乃基於歷史經
    on historical experience and other factors, including expectations of      驗及其他因素，包括可能對實體產生財務影響並
    future events that may have a financial impact on the entity and that      於此等情況下被視為合理之未來事件之預期。
    are believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.


    Deferred income tax assets                                                 遞延所得稅資產
    Deferred income tax assets are recognized for unused tax losses and        遞延所得稅資產於有可能出現可利用該等虧損及
    deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is probable         可抵扣暫時差額予以抵扣的應課稅利潤時，就未
    that taxable profit will be available against which the losses and         動用稅項虧損及可抵扣暫時差額確認。釐定可予
    deductible temporary differences can be utilized. Significant              確認的遞延所得稅資產金額時，管理層須根據日
    management judgement is required to determine the amount of                後應課稅利潤可能產生的時間及水平，配合未來
    deferred income tax assets that can be recognized, based upon the          稅項規劃策略作出重大判斷。於2024年12月31
    likely timing and level of future taxable profits together with future     日，與已確認稅項虧損及可抵扣暫時差額相關的
    tax planning strategies. The carrying value of deferred income tax         遞延所得稅資產賬面值為人民幣99,610,000元
    assets relating to recognized tax losses and deductible temporary         （2023年：人民幣102,294,000元）。進一步詳情
    differences at 31 December 2024 was RMB99,610,000 (2023:                   載於財務報表附註31。
    RMB102,294,000). Further details are contained in Note 31 to the
    financial statements.


    Variable consideration for volume rebates                                  批量返利的可變代價
    The Group estimates variable consideration to be included in the           本集團就將計入銷售具批量返利的產品交易價格
    transaction price for the sales of products with volume rebates.           的可變代價進行估計。

    The Group’s volume rebates are analysed on a per customer basis for        本集團的批量返利乃就設有單一批量限額的合同
    contracts that are subject to a single volume threshold. Determining       按每名客戶進行分析。釐定客戶是否可能獲得返
    whether a customer is likely to be entitled to a rebate depends            利取決於客戶過往獲得返利的情況及迄今累計的
    on the customer’s historical rebate entitlement and accumulated            採購額。
    purchases to date.


    The Group updates its assessment of expected volume rebates yearly         本集團每年更新其預期批量返利評估，及就退款
    and the refund liabilities are adjusted accordingly. Estimates of          負債作相應調整。預期年度批量返利的估計對情
    expected volume rebates are sensitive to changes in circumstances          況變化相當敏感，而本集團過往有關返利支付情
    and the Group’s past experience regarding rebate entitlements may          況的經驗可能無法代表客戶未來獲得實際返利的
    not be representative of a customer’s actual rebate entitlements in        情況。
    the future.
